# RH frontier: active routes and route candidates

Sources: docs/reports/endpoints.md (259 families: 205 LIVE, 54 NO-GO); docs/reports/STATUS.md; local Lean corpus; route cards. Killed mechanisms: docs/reports/rh-killed-routes.md. [L] = cited Lean declaration; [P] = paper proof; no fresh compilation implied.

**State.** RH open. 16 active routes; 23 candidates. Route proposals 1–3: stated mechanisms refuted, residuals live (KR-R1 → TG fallback, KR-R2 → RN, KR-R3 → WD). Active routes have RH-strength receivers, no source producer (STATUS F01, CANNOT-24, CANNOT-192r).

**Terms.** Receiver = proved implication/equivalence ending in RH whose premise is open. Producer = proof of such a premise from source data. Route = a receiver plus a named producer mechanism that is not excluded, plus one exact open step. Candidate = interface or fragment without a handoff to a receiver or without an independent open step. Killed = every proposed producer of the route is impossible, circular (premise = RH) or nonselective (see the killed ledger). "Active" does not mean promising.

**Codes.** F#### = endpoint family (endpoints.md). MUST-/CANNOT-/F-n/FAIL-n = STATUS.md entries (suffix `r` = reused number). KR-x, no-go-NNN = killed ledger entries. R:X = formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower/X.lean; H:X = formal/GaloisForLFunctions/HTGeometry/X.lean; Pn:x = docs/drafts/pipeline/{0-backlog,1-in-progress,2-fully-proven,3-formalized,4-publication-ready,5-failed}[n]/x.md; ULF:x = P1:universal-law-field/x. Tags: [L] Lean, [P] paper proof only, [N] numerical only.

**Legacy labels → routes.** C1→RW · CCM-FU→FU · CLG/TTF, K-RVM, S6F, TCD, UEB→TH · CSB, STC, U, MPLF, RHist, BPC, TQFQ, WS2, SQB→RN · D2, Q, RG, RG-ETA, TK→PA · E, EDQ, L, HDL, PSRC→MC · FZZ-P, PW-B→PM · G, HC→WD · LI→LI · O→WL + RW (genus part killed: KR-GEN) · P, SP-E→RP · SV2, RG-HF→SZ · T1, T2, Route T→TT · Route T successor / ULF direct sub-unit anchor→TG · Deninger lanes→HF. Retired: HB, AKC, TMD (killed ledger).

## Gates every route must pass

Distilled from STATUS; each is a proved obstruction, not a preference.
- Sharp endpoints. de Bruijn–Newman Λ=0; a₁ is approached along every base-power orbit and along actual primes. No smaller sharp lag ceiling is possible; bounded lags with ANY finite ceiling still imply RH. Log-height free-orbit count bounds cannot supply the uniform sub-quantum threshold (CANNOT-437); von Koch logarithms concern different ψ/π normalizations. E: R:WeilLagFunctionRecurrence; R:WeilLagLocalWorkHuxley; R:VonKochChebyshevBound; R:VonKochPrimeCounting.
- Exact primes. If `Λ+w` is Weil-positive and `Σ_{k≤n}w(k)=κ₁(n−1)+κ₂(√n−1)+E(n)`, `Σ|E(n)|/(n√n)<∞`, then `w=0` (MUST-507). In particular, nonzero `ℓ¹(n⁻¹ᐟ²)` or partial-sum `O(n^θ)`, `θ<½`, perturbations fail (MUST-502/503); weights supported on `O(N^θ)` positions, `θ<½`, fail. Sieve majorants, sparse weights and regular residue/prime-power shifts cannot replace exact weights. Full-cone `lam−Λ=o(Λ)` also forces lam=Λ [L], including bounded prime-only deviations (R:WeilLittleOVonMangoldtRigidity).
- Rigidity, not estimate. Any inequality whose hypotheses survive a single-prime Euler-factor deformation gives neither RH nor a zero-free region nor an off-line count (MUST-389; CANNOT-64/65).
- Orientation. The separating datum is reflection-odd and two-torsion; reflection-even real readers, localized/equivariant invariants and averages are blind to it (CANNOT-399, CANNOT-405/407, CANNOT-425, MUST-433).
- Individual zeros. Unconditional arithmetic identities see only pair-even smoothed densities; no fixed functional individuates a zero (CANNOT-182r).
- Exponent, not constant. The missing step moves an exponent by ½ at once; no method gives any exponent below ½ in the lag/Weil lanes (MUST-250, MUST-261).
- Cofinal source law. Finite data, fixed jets and finite packets are nonselective; the law must be proved on pre-divisor source data before specializing at Xi zeros (CANNOT-02, MUST-01, MUST-29/30). Any finite holomorphic decaying test family with an invertible line-pair derivative matrix admits a distant off-line quadruple plus support slides preserving every test value [L]; regular-tuple hypothesis retained (R:CriticalLocusRegularTupleBlindness).
- Mellin-diagonal inputs are blind. Dilations, dyadic/prime-2 self-similarity and the Hecke semigroup act on Mellin transforms by units or characters; common eigenvectors include Helson series with off-line zeros; together with the functional equation they pin ζ and leave no deformation handle (KR-R2).
- Holomorphic readers are unquantized. Zero-free anchored holomorphic approximants with bounded boundary argument have zero-free limits [L] (R:AnchoredPhasePassageRigidity); a holomorphic clock unimodular on the line gives one growing mode per free orbit (CANNOT-458). Source realizations of zero-side quantized forms are 0/∞ growth criteria: sub-unit margins are idle, any finite bound is RH; the open core is finiteness at the `√X` scale (KR-R1, KR-R3; CANNOT-461).

## Active routes

### WL — Weil lag function (legacy O)
Target: RH ⇔ `Φ(L)=Σ'_ρ e^{L(ρ−½)}/(ρ(1−ρ))` bounded; one side suffices; sharp form `a_n=Φ(log n) ≤ a_1=2+γ−log 4π` ∀n [L]. F0130.
Proved [L]: either one-sided bound `±D(T)≤B e^{T/2}` for `D(T)=∫₀ᵀ(e^t−ψ(e^t))dt` forces RH. E: R:ChebyshevDeficitPrimitiveOneSidedRH.
Proved [L]: dichotomy — all `a_n ∈ [−a_1,a_1]` or `(a_n)` unbounded both ways; `|Φ(L)| ≤ Ce^{L/2}e^{−cL^{1/10}}` unconditionally; `Φ ≤ Me^{aL}` ⇒ every `Re ρ ≤ ½+a`; bounded prime lags ⇒ `¼ ≤ Re ρ ≤ ¾` (Hoheisel); `Re G ≥ 0` on `Re s>½` unconditionally (reaches only `Re ρ ≤ 1`); Frobenius-orbit form `B(v_x,v_y)=Φ(log x−log y)`, RH ⇔ `B ≥ 0` on that span; Gaussian Euler–continuum passage: bounded `L¹` boundary argument of `Lₓ+Aₓ` on circles `C(1+iγ,r)`, `0<r<½`, along one sequence `x_k→0⁺` ⇒ RH (R:AnchoredPhasePassageRigidity); RH ⇔ holomorphic two-prime lag excess bounded ∀T (R:XiHolomorphicTwoPrimePencilGrowthDichotomy).
Proved [L]: RH ⇔ actual prime lags bounded below ⇔ eventually bounded below; upper and lower prime receivers both closed. Unconditionally, prime lags approach a₁ cofinally and fall below some fixed −η<0 cofinally. Neither oscillation supplies a bound. E: R:PrimeLagLowerBoundRH.
Proved [L, arithmetic receivers]: RH ⇔ eventual Nicolas primorial inequality ⇔ eventual Robin divisor-sum inequality ⇔ eventual Robin inequality on lcm(1,…,n) ⇔ eventual Lagarias harmonic inequality. No Θ<1 premise. Under RH, scaled Nicolas defect equals `2+Φ(log x)+o(1)`, with sharp/cofinal prime upper boundary 2+a₁. Independent inequalities remain open. E: R:NicolasInequalityRiemannConverse; R:NicolasScaledDefectRH; R:RobinCriterionRiemann; R:RobinLcmRiemannCriterion; R:LagariasCriterionRiemann.
Open: one one-sided prime inequality at scale `√X`: `√X∫_X^∞(ψ(t)−t+log 2π+½log(1−t⁻²))t⁻²dt ≥ −C` (or the reverse), i.e. `∫_1^p m(t)dt ≤ C√p` at primes. Named producer shape: an archimedean Hodge index — intersection pairing on principal divisors of Spec ℤ with regularized values `Φ(log(n/m))` and Hodge inequality `Φ(0) ≥ Φ(L)` (MUST-229/231/236); residuals of routes 1 and 3 land here (KR-R1, KR-R3).
Proved [L, unconditional]: for `2≤n≤k≤m`, `a_k≤max(0,a_n,a_m)+(m−n)²/(8n√k)`; the alternative `(m−n)²/(4k√k)` bound also holds. Positive actual-prime ramps and convexity of the full trivial-zero correction give upper interpolation across every arithmetic interval. Any integer set bracketing each `k≥2` by `n,m` with `(m−n)²≤C n√n` is RH-complete for bounded upper lags, with global ceiling `max(M,0)+C/8`; arbitrary `n^{3/4}` meshes need no counting-series continuation or attained zero abscissa. E: R:WeilLagQuadraticInterpolation (`integerLag_curvature_upper_interpolation`, `quadraticLagMesh_characterization`).

Proved [L]: exact scaled lag chord deficit = logarithmic defect J − nonnegative triangular prime charge Q − nonnegative trivial-zero deficit. Wider meshes are RH-complete when J−Q≤C√k and upper sample lags are bounded. Quadratic meshes satisfy the vanishing-work law below. Open: payment certificates beyond this mesh class; actual sample-lag ceiling. E: R:WeilLagQuadraticInterpolation (`sqrt_integerLag_chord_exact`, `primeCompensatedLagMesh_characterization`).
Proved [L, local work]: for 2≤n≤k≤m, u,v≥0, u+v=1, k=un+vm, `J−Q=un∫_n^k(ψ(t)−t−c)t⁻²dt−vm∫_k^m(ψ(t)−t−c)t⁻²dt`, every constant c. Unconditional short-interval control with c=ψ(k)−k gives local work o(√k), uniformly on k/2≤n≤k≤m, (m−n)²≤Ck^(3/2), fixed C≥0. Hence ∀ε>0 eventually `a_k≤max(0,a_n,a_m)+ε`. Local-work estimate closed; upper sample-lag/signed-mean producer open. E: R:WeilLagLocalChebyshevChord; R:WeilLagLocalWorkHuxley (`abs_localWork_le`, `eventually_integerLag_le_max_add_of_quadraticMesh`).
Proved [L]: actual primes form a quadratic integer-lag mesh via unconditional 71/100 gaps, with a fixed prime covering the initial segment. Bounded upper prime lags ⇔ RH, sharp ceiling a₁; one-sided prime growth exponent sharp. Mesh geometry supplies no sample-lag ceiling. E: R:WeilLagPrimeQuadraticMesh; R:PrimeLagLandauPrimesOnly; R:PrimeLagGrowthExponent.
Proved [L, unconditional]: for every finite probability mean on integer nodes `n≥r≥2` with integer barycenter `k`, `√k a_k≤Σ w_n√n a_n+Σ w_n(n−k)²/(2r)`. Fixed geometric mean schedules with variance `≤C r√k` are RH-complete for bounded upper normalized signed means, with global ceiling `M+C/2`. This permits negative lag values to contribute to the receiver; no individual sample ceiling is required. Open: a source proof bounding the actual signed means on one such schedule. E: R:WeilLagBarycentricMean (`sqrt_integerLag_le_finite_mean_add_variance`, `barycentricLagMeanScheme_characterization`); P1:wl-barycentric-signed-lag-mean-2026-09-29.
Proved [L, excursions]: fixed D>0, ε>0, eventually a_k>ε forces one entire adjacent side of radius Dk^(3/4) to have lag ≥a_k−ε; left side restricted to n≥k/2. For every ε>0, infinitely many actual primes satisfy a_p>a₁−ε. No strictly smaller eventual prime ceiling; no upper ceiling proved. Open: a source upper sample bound or opposing low-lag population. E: R:WeilLagExcursionBlocks; R:WeilLagLocalWorkHuxley (`eventually_integerLag_block`, `frequently_prime_integerLag_gt`).
Closed around it: uniform-gap producers and finite prime truncations (MUST-234/245); de la Vallée Poussin positivity (CANNOT-341); one base orbit or sparse sampling support (CANNOT-327/328); self-pairings (CANNOT-325); lag Gram/sign shortcuts (no-go-149, 441); genus fixed part (KR-GEN).
E: R:WeilLagFunctionInteger; R:WeilLagFunctionRecurrence; R:WeilLagFunctionOmega; R:PrimeLagLandauSeries; R:LagPairThresholdSharp; P1:integer-lag-law-notes; R:AnchoredPhasePassageRigidity; R:XiHolomorphicTwoPrimePencilGrowthDichotomy.

For every sufficiently large a and α>0, every valid scalar certificate `α−∫GR≤−(π/96)e^a/a`: G≥0, α−G≤S_a, R bounds Fourier density on all pole-vanishing physical vectors, GR integrable. Proved [L]: uncompressed finite-prime transport `T_a` on `L²(−a,a)` has `‖T_a‖=(1+o(1))e^a`; pole-profile weighted Schur row = Chebyshev `ψ` + reciprocal-Mangoldt sum; elementary bound `‖T_a‖≤(log 4)e^a+4ae^{a/2}+2a+log 4+4`. ## Falsification routes

A disproof needs one certified value of the wrong sign. Lean-certified falsifier forms (endpoints §FAL and falsifier clauses of live families):
- Localized Hermite form `Re Σc_ic_j p_{i+j+2}(t)<0` at an off-spectral base point; high odd monomial at a zero's ordinate (F0125). Decides an isolated off-line zero by one derivative evaluation; the isolation hypothesis cannot be removed (MUST-432). E: R:LocalHermitePositivity; R:TraceFormSignature.
- Negative shifted reciprocal moment or Hankel quadratic (F0260); negative completed squared-ξ source jet (F0196); negative Gaussian heat trace, Turán minor or heat-Hankel minor (F0103).
- One real `t` with `Ξ'(t)²<Ξ(t)Ξ''(t)` (F0240); positive completed-flux quadratic coefficient on the line (F0047).
- One multiple zero of de Bruijn H_t at positive time refutes RH [L]; negative-time collisions do not. E: R:DeBruijnNewmanCollisionCriterion.
- One finite certified prime-side Pick witness at a positive shift (F0257); one finite continued Cauchy packet with negative source energy (F0274).
- Rouché sphere domination planting a zero in `Re s>½` (F0150); an odd-order right-half zero (F0190); integral faithful Arakelov coordinates (F0089).
- Violating any of 63 standalone consequences of RH (F0276), e.g. `ψ(N)−N=O(√N log²N)`, `Re ξ'/ξ>0` on `Re s>½`, Li positivity, lag bound `|Φ| ≤ Φ(0)`.
Limits: numerical PSD checks never outrun the verified zero-free height (MUST-245); conditional level-`S` Hodge positivity excludes fixed-depth zeros up to `T≈exp(c_δ S)` but does not supply that positivity or exclude arbitrarily shallow zeros (MUST-475–477); paired Li coefficients are never eventually negative, only both-signed infinitely often (CANNOT-194r).

## Unconditional infrastructure (not routes)

- Product-divisor cosine repair [L, failed construction with analytic survivor]: cancelling the diagonal basepoint of `(H+iK)/(H-iK)`, H=T(w)-T(-w), K=(T(w)-T(0))*(T(-w)-T(0)), gives both reflections, a simple holomorphic 1-point at zero, homogeneous 1-fiber containment in 3ℤ, no nonzero projective period, infinitely many regular 1-points and no rational representation. Nevertheless BOTH other cube-root fibers have infinitely many genuine nonreal holomorphic points with nonzero denominator. Two analytic submersions construct the corner branch; exact conic/exponential identities and a local cosine inverse give an injective discrete specialization. This excludes this full C2-P candidate, including cancellations. Positive survivor is the analytic branch and its infinite genuine fibers. General rational-repair classification, C2-P and actual Xi remain open. Classical parent methods; significant novelty unverified. The reciprocal quotient of its unit-circle Mobius normalization, `G(w)/G(-w)`, satisfies both symmetries but takes the genuine holomorphic value 1 at `6*i*sqrt(5)`, violating the marked integer fiber. More generally, every bivariate polynomial norm-one repair P/Q with P(a,a)=Q(a,a)!=0 at any diagonal point has infinitely many genuine nonreal holomorphic 1-fibers: the cosine curve has infinitely many diagonal intersections with distinct coordinates. Origin regularity is deduced. The complete-fiber integer specialization is also formalized. Rescaled inverse-branch displacements stabilize exactly; no growth estimate enters. No dilation law is supplied for arbitrary C2-P sections or Xi. Significant novelty unverified. - Marked Legendre/Humbert geometry [L/P, bridge]: over `C(q)`, the explicit section of `E_((1+2q²)/(1+q²))` is nonsingular and not twice any rational point [L: H:LegendreSourceKummer]; this does not establish non-divisibility after Xi pullback. Actual exponentially marked Xi germs have exactly the relations obtained by grouping monomials with equal degree and exponential parameter; the whole prime family and every pair with distinct parameters are algebraically independent [L: R:XiExponentialSourceRelations]. Arithmetic kernel and image [L: R:XiIntegerSourceToricIdeal; H:HomogenizedMonoidAlgebra; H:HomogenizedMonoidPinching]: for actual upper-end germs `d_n=xi*n^(-s)`, the relation ideal is exactly `(X_a X_b-X_1 X_ab)` and the image is the pinched monoid algebra `C+t*C[N_{>0}][t]`. Unique prime factorization identifies this with `C+t*C[x_p][t]` [L: H:HomogenizedIntegerBlowup; R:XiIntegerSourceToricIdeal]. Reflection and all cancellations still admit an exact real entire countermodel with an entire cubic lift for integer mark 2 and off-line zeros of exact analytic order two [L: H:ReflectedCubicExponentialModel]; it is differentially algebraic and does not model Xi hypertranscendence.
